> DEVELOPER

I want rename this app to "Ovid", the TUI version rename to "Ovid TUI" or "Ovid CLI", what do you think?

> AGENT

Good naming instinct. A few thoughts:

**"Ovid" for the desktop app** — makes sense. The GUI is the primary, polished product that end users interact with. Giving it the canonical name signals it's the flagship.

**"Ovid TUI" vs "Ovid CLI"**
- **TUI** is more accurate technically (it has a full terminal UI, not just command-line flags), but less recognizable to non-technical users
- **CLI** is more universally understood and searchable (people google "CLI tool" more than "TUI tool")
- If the TUI has a rich interactive interface (not just `ovid --flag`), **Ovid TUI** is the more honest name

My recommendation: **Ovid** + **Ovid TUI**. It's accurate and draws a clean distinction between "desktop app" and "terminal app" rather than implying one is a lesser command-line wrapper.

One thing to consider: do you want to rename the repo `ovid-app` → `ovid` and the TUI repo → `ovid-tui`? That would be consistent, though it's a bigger change (git remote URLs, CI, etc.).

> DEVELOPER

or what do you think about the name of TUI version, do you have any other ideas?

> AGENT

Since the project has a classical literary theme (Ovid the Roman poet, Amytis the historical figure), staying in that space makes sense. A few ideas:

**Classical poets / Roman writers (same era as Ovid):**
- **Virgil** — my top pick. Virgil is Dante's *guide* through the underworld — a perfect metaphor for navigating your content in a terminal. Same Roman era, well-known, short.
- **Horace** — another Augustan-era poet, close contemporary of Ovid. Less obvious than Virgil.
- **Seneca** — Roman writer/philosopher, slightly later. Strong name, less poetic connotation.

**Thematic fits:**
- **Hermes** — messenger, speed, text transmission. Fits a terminal tool well.
- **Scribe** — functional, clear, but generic.

**My recommendation: `Virgil`**

It earns its name on two levels — Roman poet (consistent with Ovid) and literary guide (Dante's navigator) — which maps cleanly onto "a terminal tool that helps you navigate your writing workspace." It's also short, easy to type, and memorable.

`ovid` (desktop) + `virgil` (TUI) would be a clean pairing.

> DEVELOPER

Another question: it seems like we are putting a lot of effort into rendering Markdown elements. Why not use Tiptap Markdown or another Markdown component?

> AGENT

They actually are using `tiptap-markdown` — it's listed in CLAUDE.md as the serialization/deserialization layer. The custom extension work in `src/lib/tiptap/` isn't reimplementing Markdown rendering, it's building UX features on top:

- `FindReplace.ts` — find & replace with live highlighting
- `TextFolding.ts` — fold/unfold headings via chevron widgets
- `InlineEditMode.ts` — show raw `[text](url)` decorations when cursor is inside a link
- `LinkPreview.ts` — hover tooltip for links

These have no off-the-shelf equivalent in the tiptap ecosystem.

The deeper reason the app can't just use a Markdown renderer (like `react-markdown`) is the **Typora-style WYSIWYG** design goal — markdown renders inline as you type, with no split pane. That requires a full rich-text editor that understands the document model and can serialize back to clean `.md`. Tiptap + ProseMirror + `tiptap-markdown` is the right tool for that; a renderer alone only reads, it doesn't write.

So the stack is: `tiptap-markdown` handles the markdown in/out, Tiptap/ProseMirror handles editing, and the custom extensions add the UX polish on top.

I've conducted a thorough search of the repository for all image-related code. Here's the complete breakdown:

Supported formats: PNG, JPG, JPEG, GIF, WebP, AVIF, SVG+XML

### 3. Image Drop Handling (Drag & Drop Upload)

**File:** `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/ovid-app/src/components/Editor.tsx` (Lines 129-167)

The `handleDrop` editorProps function:
- Filters dropped files by MIME type using `IMAGE_MIME` regex
- Extracts file name and system path from dropped files
- Calls Tauri command `save_asset` for each image file
- Passes `activeFilePath` (current editor file path) to compute relative paths
- Batches uploads with `Promise.allSettled` for async processing
- Recomputes drop position after uploads complete to avoid stale offsets
- Inserts image nodes with relative paths as `src` attribute

### 4. Tauri Backend: Asset Saving

**File:** `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/ovid-app/src-tauri/src/lib.rs` (Lines 639-707)

**Function:** `save_asset(src_path: String, active_file_path: Option<String>)`

Key details:
- **Source path validation:** Intentionally NOT validated against workspace root (allows drag-drop from external sources like desktop)
- **Destination:** `<workspace_root>/assets/` directory (created if missing)
- **Atomic reservation:** Uses `OpenOptions::new().write(true).create_new(true)` for TOCTOU-safe file creation
- **Collision handling:** Falls back to timestamp-prefixed names (e.g., `1710608000_image.png`) if filename exists
- **Relative path calculation:** Uses `relative_path_from()` function (lines 625-637) to compute paths:
  - If `active_file_path` provided: returns path relative to the active file's directory
  - Fallback: returns `assets/<filename>`
- **Registered command:** Line 923 in `invoke_handler`

- `@tiptap/extension-image: ^3.20.1` - Tiptap image node extension

### 7. Rust Backend Dependencies

### 8. Site Config Parsing (Amytis Workspace Detection)

**File:** `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/ovid-app/src-tauri/src/lib.rs` (Lines 426-513)

The `parse_content_types()` function extracts content types from `site.config.ts`:
- **Config location check:** Line 224, 271 - looks for `site.config.ts` in workspace root
- **Amytis workspace detection:** Requires both `site.config.ts` AND `content/` directory
- No specific image CDN or asset configuration extraction (only content types are parsed)

### 9. Workspace Structure

The app distinguishes between:
- **workspace_root:** The actual workspace directory (where `site.config.ts` lives)
- **tree_root:** Preferentially `content/` subdirectory if it exists; fallback to workspace root
- **Assets location:** Always `<workspace_root>/assets/` regardless of tree_root

### 10. No CDN Integration

- No CDN configuration is parsed or used
- All images remain local files in the `assets/` directory
- Image URLs are relative paths computed at drop time
- No image transformation or optimization pipeline

### Key Architecture Points

1. **Graceful degradation:** Asset saving uses `Promise.allSettled`, so partial failures don't crash the entire operation
2. **Path safety:** Rust side strictly validates file paths against workspace root for reading/writing, but intentionally allows external sources for asset imports
3. **Typora-style WYSIWYG:** Images render inline as you edit (via Tiptap)
4. **Relative paths:** Images are stored relative to the active file's directory when possible, enabling portable workspaces

> AGENT

Good find. Here's the current situation and what's needed:

## Current State

Images **are** inserted correctly (drop handling works, relative paths are computed, saved to `assets/`). They just **don't render** because Tauri's WebView can't load file system paths like `../assets/foo.png` directly — they need to go through Tauri's `convertFileSrc()` (which rewrites them to `asset://localhost/...`).

## The Three Path Cases

| Source | Example | Resolution |
|---|---|---|
| Relative | `./images/foo.png` | Resolve against current file's dir → `convertFileSrc()` |
| Root-relative | `/images/foo.png` | Resolve against workspace root → `convertFileSrc()` |
| CDN-prefixed | `/images/foo.png` (with CDN) | Prepend CDN base URL, return as-is (HTTP) |
| External | `https://...` | Pass through unchanged |

## Proposed Approach

1. **Custom Image NodeView** (`src/lib/tiptap/ImageRenderer.tsx`) — a React NodeView that intercepts `src` at render time, applies the resolution logic, and renders the `<img>`. This keeps the on-disk `.md` clean (paths stay as written).

2. **Path resolution hook** — needs `currentFilePath` + `workspaceRoot` (already available in App state) passed down to the editor.

3. **CDN parsing** — extend the Rust-side `site.config.ts` parser to extract a CDN base URL (e.g. `cdn.base`) and expose it via a new or extended Tauri command. Frontend stores it in App state alongside workspace info.

## What I'd suggest deferring

- Image resize handles (out of scope for now)
- Image upload to CDN (write path; separate feature)

   294→          editor.chain().focus().toggleCode().run();
   295→          break;
   296→        case "format-heading-1":
   297→          editor.chain().focus().toggleHeading({ level: 1 }).run();
   298→          break;
   299→        case "format-heading-2":
   300→          editor.chain().focus().toggleHeading({ level: 2 }).run();
   301→          break;
   302→        case "format-heading-3":
   303→          editor.chain().focus().toggleHeading({ level: 3 }).run();
   304→          break;
   305→        case "format-blockquote":
   306→          editor.chain().focus().toggleBlockquote().run();
   307→          break;
   308→        case "format-bullet-list":
   309→          editor.chain().focus().toggleBulletList().run();
   310→          break;
   311→        case "format-ordered-list":
   312→          editor.chain().focus().toggleOrderedList().run();
   313→          break;
   314→        case "insert-link": {
   315→          const href = editor.getAttributes("link").href ?? "";
   316→          setLinkDialog({ href });
   317→          break;
   318→        }
   319→        case "insert-code-block":
   320→          editor.chain().focus().toggleCodeBlock().run();
   321→          break;
   322→        case "insert-hr":
   323→          editor.chain().focus().setHorizontalRule().run();
   324→          break;
   325→        case "insert-table":
   326→          editor.chain().focus().insertTable({ rows: 3, cols: 3, withHeaderRow: true }).run();
   327→          break;
   328→      }
   329→    }).then((fn) => {
   330→      if (mounted) {
   331→        unlisten = fn;
   332→      } else {
   333→        fn();
   334→      }
   335→    });
   336→    return () => {
   337→      mounted = false;
   338→      unlisten?.();
   339→    };
   340→  }, [editor, linkDialog]);
   341→
   342→  return (
   343→    <div className="editor-wrapper">
   344→      <div ref={scrollRef} className="editor-scroll">
   345→        <EditorContent editor={editor} />
   346→      </div>
   347→      {editor && showFindReplace && (
   348→        <FindReplaceBar
   349→          editor={editor}
   350→          onClose={() => {
   351→            setShowFindReplace(false);
   352→            editor.chain().focus().run();
   353→          }}
   354→        />
   355→      )}
   356→      {editor && <TableControls editor={editor} />}
   357→      {editor && (
   358→        <BubbleMenu
   359→          editor={editor}
   360→          onLinkClick={() => {
   361→            const href = editor.getAttributes("link").href ?? "";
   362→            setLinkDialog({ href });
   363→          }}
   364→        />
   365→      )}
   366→      {linkDialog && (
   367→        <LinkDialog
   368→          initialHref={linkDialog.href}
   369→          onApply={(url) => {
   370→            editor?.chain().focus().extendMarkRange("link").setLink({ href: url }).run();
   371→            setLinkDialog(null);
   372→          }}
   373→          onRemove={() => {
   374→            editor?.chain().focus().extendMarkRange("link").unsetLink().run();
   375→            setLinkDialog(null);
   376→          }}
   377→          onCancel={() => setLinkDialog(null)}
   378→        />
   379→      )}
   380→    </div>
   381→  );
   382→}

   204→async fn open_workspace_at_path(
   205→    path: String,
   206→    state: State<'_, WorkspaceState>,
   207→) -> Result<Option<WorkspaceResult>, String> {
   208→    let root = PathBuf::from(&path);
   209→    if !root.is_dir() {
   210→        return Ok(None);
   211→    }
   212→
   213→    let name = root
   214→        .file_name()
   215→        .map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().to_string())
   216→        .unwrap_or_else(|| root.to_string_lossy().to_string());
   217→
   218→    let content_dir = root.join("content");
   219→    let tree_root = if content_dir.is_dir() { content_dir } else { root.clone() };
   220→
   221→    *state.tree_root.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())? = Some(tree_root.clone());
   222→    *state.workspace_root.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())? = Some(root.clone());

   230→        tree_root: tree_root.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
   231→        tree,
   232→        is_amytis_workspace,
   233→    }))
   234→}

   420→#[derive(Serialize)]
   421→#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
   422→struct ContentType {
   423→    name: String,
   424→}
   425→
   426→/// Best-effort scanner: find `contentTypes` in `site.config.ts` and extract
   427→/// the top-level key names (post, page, note, …). Returns empty vec on any
   428→/// parse failure so callers gracefully degrade to the default frontmatter.
   429→fn parse_content_types(config_path: &Path) -> Vec<ContentType> {
   430→    use std::io::{BufRead, BufReader};
   431→    let Ok(file) = std::fs::File::open(config_path) else {
   432→        return Vec::new();
   433→    };
   434→    let mut reader = BufReader::new(file);
   435→    let mut type_names: Vec<ContentType> = Vec::new();
   436→    let mut depth: i32 = 0;
   437→    let mut found_content_types = false;
   438→    let mut content_types_depth: Option<i32> = None;
   439→    let mut line = String::new();
   440→
   441→    loop {
   442→        line.clear();
   443→        if reader.read_line(&mut line).unwrap_or(0) == 0 {
   444→            break;
   445→        }
   446→        let trimmed = line.trim();
   447→        let open_count = line.chars().filter(|&c| c == '{').count() as i32;
   448→        let close_count = line.chars().filter(|&c| c == '}').count() as i32;
   449→
   450→        match content_types_depth {
   451→            None if !found_content_types => {
   452→                if trimmed.contains("contentTypes") {
   453→                    found_content_types = true;
   454→                    depth += open_count - close_count;
   455→                    if open_count > 0 {
   456→                        content_types_depth = Some(depth);
   457→                    }
   458→                } else {
   459→                    depth += open_count - close_count;
   460→                }
   461→            }
   462→            None => {
   463→                // found keyword, waiting for opening brace
   464→                depth += open_count - close_count;
   465→                if open_count > 0 {
   466→                    content_types_depth = Some(depth);
   467→                }
   468→            }
   469→            Some(ct_depth) => {
   470→                let prev_depth = depth;
   471→                depth += open_count - close_count;
   472→                if depth < ct_depth {
   473→                    break;
   474→                }
   475→                if prev_depth == ct_depth {
   476→                    if let Some(name) = extract_ts_key(trimmed) {
   477→                        type_names.push(ContentType { name });
   478→                    }
   479→                }
   480→            }
   481→        }
   482→    }
   483→    type_names
   484→}
   485→
   486→fn extract_ts_key(line: &str) -> Option<String> {
   487→    let line = line.trim();
   488→    if line.starts_with("//") || line.starts_with('*') || line.starts_with("/*") {
   489→        return None;
   490→    }
   491→    let end = line.find(':')?;
   492→    let key = line[..end].trim().trim_matches('"').trim_matches('\'');
   493→    if !key.is_empty() && key.chars().all(|c| c.is_alphanumeric() || c == '_') {
   494→        Some(key.to_string())
   495→    } else {
   496→        None
   497→    }
   498→}
   499→
   500→#[tauri::command]
   501→fn get_content_types(state: State<'_, WorkspaceState>) -> Result<Vec<ContentType>, String> {
   502→    let root_guard = state.workspace_root.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   503→    let root = match root_guard.as_ref() {
   504→        Some(r) => r.clone(),
   505→        None => return Ok(Vec::new()),
   506→    };
   507→    drop(root_guard);
   508→    let config = root.join("site.config.ts");
   509→    if !config.is_file() {
   510→        return Ok(Vec::new());
   511→    }
   512→    Ok(parse_content_types(&config))
   513→}
   514→
   515→// ── Git integration ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   516→
   517→#[derive(Serialize)]
   518→#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
   519→struct GitFileStatus {
   520→    path: String,
   521→    status: String,
   522→}
   523→
   524→/// Run a git subcommand rooted at `root`. Returns stdout on success or an
   525→/// error string (stderr) on failure. Returns an empty string if git is not
   526→/// found, so callers can treat a missing git as a graceful no-op.
   527→fn run_git(root: &str, args: &[&str]) -> Result<String, String> {
   528→    let mut cmd_args = vec!["-C", root];
   529→    cmd_args.extend_from_slice(args);
   530→    let output = std::process::Command::new("git")
   531→        .args(&cmd_args)
   532→        .output()
   533→        .map_err(|_| "git not found".to_string())?;
   534→    if !output.status.success() {
   535→        let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).trim().to_string();
   536→        return Err(if stderr.is_empty() { "git command failed".to_string() } else { stderr });
   537→    }
   538→    Ok(String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout).to_string())
   539→}

     1→import { invoke } from "@tauri-apps/api/core";
     2→import type { MutableRefObject } from "react";
     3→import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
     4→import { createAmytisFrontmatter, createTypedFrontmatter } from "./frontmatter";
     5→import type { FileNode } from "./types";
     6→
     7→export interface WorkspaceResult {
     8→  name: string;
     9→  rootPath: string;
    10→  treeRoot: string;
    11→  tree: FileNode[];
    12→  isAmytisWorkspace: boolean;
    13→}
    14→
    15→interface UseWorkspaceOptions {
    16→  showToast: (msg: string) => void;
    17→  flushPendingSave: () => Promise<void>;
    18→  handleCloseFile: () => Promise<void>;
    19→  handleSelectFile: (node: FileNode) => Promise<void>;
    20→  selectedFile: FileNode | null;
    21→  selectedPathRef: MutableRefObject<string | null>;
    22→  setSelectedFile: (node: FileNode | null) => void;
    23→  resetFileState: () => void;
    24→}
    25→
    26→function findNode(nodes: FileNode[], path: string): FileNode | undefined {
    27→  for (const n of nodes) {
    28→    if (n.path === path) return n;
    29→    if (n.children) {
    30→      const found = findNode(n.children, path);
    31→      if (found) return found;
    32→    }
    33→  }
    34→}
    35→
    36→export function useWorkspace({
    37→  showToast,
    38→  flushPendingSave,
    39→  handleCloseFile,
    40→  handleSelectFile,
    41→  selectedFile,
    42→  selectedPathRef,
    43→  setSelectedFile,
    44→  resetFileState,
    45→}: UseWorkspaceOptions) {
    46→  const [tree, setTree] = useState<FileNode[]>([]);
    47→  const [workspaceName, setWorkspaceName] = useState<string | null>(null);
    48→  const [workspaceRoot, setWorkspaceRoot] = useState<string | null>(null);
    49→  const [workspaceRootPath, setWorkspaceRootPath] = useState<string | null>(null);
    50→  const [isAmytisWorkspace, setIsAmytisWorkspace] = useState(false);
    51→  const [renamingPath, setRenamingPath] = useState<string | null>(null);
    52→
    53→  const refreshTree = useCallback(async (): Promise<FileNode[]> => {
    54→    try {
    55→      const updated = await invoke<FileNode[]>("list_workspace");
    56→      setTree(updated);
    57→      return updated;
    58→    } catch (err) {
    59→      console.error("Failed to refresh tree:", err);
    60→      return [];
    61→    }
    62→  }, []);
    63→
    64→  const applyWorkspaceResult = useCallback(
    65→    (result: WorkspaceResult) => {
    66→      setTree(result.tree);
    67→      setWorkspaceName(result.name);
    68→      setWorkspaceRoot(result.treeRoot);
    69→      setWorkspaceRootPath(result.rootPath);
    70→      setIsAmytisWorkspace(result.isAmytisWorkspace);
    71→      resetFileState();
    72→      if (!result.isAmytisWorkspace) {
    73→        showToast("This folder doesn't look like an Amytis workspace.");
    74→      }
    75→    },
    76→    [resetFileState, showToast]
    77→  );
    78→
    79→  const openWorkspaceAtPath = useCallback(
    80→    async (path: string) => {
    81→      await flushPendingSave();
    82→      try {
    83→        const result = await invoke<WorkspaceResult | null>("open_workspace_at_path", { path });
    84→        if (result) applyWorkspaceResult(result);
    85→        else showToast("Could not open workspace — path may no longer be valid.");
    86→      } catch (err) {
    87→        console.error("Failed to open workspace:", err);
    88→        showToast(`Failed to open workspace: ${err}`);
    89→      }
    90→    },
    91→    [flushPendingSave, showToast, applyWorkspaceResult]
    92→  );
    93→
    94→  const handleOpenWorkspace = useCallback(async () => {
    95→    await flushPendingSave();
    96→    try {
    97→      const result = await invoke<WorkspaceResult | null>("open_workspace");
    98→      if (result) applyWorkspaceResult(result);
    99→    } catch (err) {
   100→      console.error("Failed to open workspace:", err);
   101→      showToast(`Failed to open workspace: ${err}`);
   102→    }
   103→  }, [flushPendingSave, showToast, applyWorkspaceResult]);
   104→
   105→  async function handleNewFile(dirPath: string, filename: string, contentType?: string) {
   106→    const slug = filename.replace(/\.md$/, "");
   107→    const filePath = `${dirPath}/${slug}.md`;
   108→    const content = contentType
   109→      ? createTypedFrontmatter(slug, contentType)
   110→      : createAmytisFrontmatter(slug);
   111→    try {
   112→      await invoke("create_file", { path: filePath, content });
   113→      const updated = await refreshTree();
   114→      const newNode = findNode(updated, filePath);
   115→      if (newNode) await handleSelectFile(newNode);
   116→    } catch (err) {
   117→      console.error("Failed to create file:", err);
   118→      showToast(`Failed to create file: ${err}`);
   119→    }
   120→  }
   121→
   122→  async function handleRename(node: FileNode, newName: string) {
   123→    setRenamingPath(null);
   124→    await flushPendingSave();
   125→    const dir = node.path.substring(0, node.path.lastIndexOf("/"));
   126→    const ext = node.extension ?? ".md";
   127→    const newPath = `${dir}/${newName}${newName.endsWith(ext) ? "" : ext}`;
   128→    try {
   129→      await invoke("rename_file", { oldPath: node.path, newPath });
   130→      const updated = await refreshTree();
   131→      if (selectedFile?.path === node.path) {
   132→        const renamed = findNode(updated, newPath);
   133→        if (renamed) {
   134→          selectedPathRef.current = newPath;
   135→          setSelectedFile(renamed);
   136→        }
   137→      }
   138→    } catch (err) {
   139→      console.error("Failed to rename file:", err);
   140→      showToast(`Failed to rename: ${err}`);
   141→    }
   142→  }
   143→
   144→  async function handleDelete(node: FileNode) {
   145→    const confirmed = window.confirm(`Move "${node.name}" to Trash?`);
   146→    if (!confirmed) return;
   147→    if (selectedFile?.path === node.path) {
   148→      await flushPendingSave();
   149→    }
   150→    try {
   151→      await invoke("trash_file", { path: node.path });
   152→      if (selectedFile?.path === node.path) await handleCloseFile();
   153→      await refreshTree();
   154→    } catch (err) {
   155→      console.error("Failed to delete file:", err);
   156→      showToast(`Failed to delete: ${err}`);
   157→    }
   158→  }
   159→
   160→  return {
   161→    tree,
   162→    workspaceName,
   163→    workspaceRoot,
   164→    workspaceRootPath,
   165→    isAmytisWorkspace,
   166→    renamingPath,
   167→    setRenamingPath,
   168→    handleOpenWorkspace,
   169→    openWorkspaceAtPath,
   170→    handleNewFile,
   171→    handleRename,
   172→    handleDelete,
   173→  };
   174→}
   175→

   623→
   624→/// Compute a POSIX-style relative path from `from_dir` to `to`.
   625→fn relative_path_from(from_dir: &Path, to: &Path) -> String {
   626→    let from: Vec<_> = from_dir.components().collect();
   627→    let to_c: Vec<_> = to.components().collect();
   628→    let common = from.iter().zip(to_c.iter()).take_while(|(a, b)| a == b).count();
   629→    let mut rel = PathBuf::new();
   630→    for _ in common..from.len() {
   631→        rel.push("..");
   632→    }
   633→    for c in &to_c[common..] {
   634→        rel.push(c);
   635→    }
   636→    rel.to_string_lossy().replace('\\', "/")
   637→}
   638→
   639→/// Copy an image from an arbitrary path into `<workspace>/assets/` and return
   640→/// a path relative to the active markdown file (or `assets/<name>` as fallback).
   641→///
   642→/// Note: unlike `read_file`/`write_file`, `src_path` is intentionally not
   643→/// validated against the workspace root — drag-and-drop sources originate from
   644→/// external locations (desktop, downloads, etc.). The destination is safely
   645→/// constrained to `<workspace>/assets/`.
   646→#[tauri::command]
   647→fn save_asset(
   648→    src_path: String,
   649→    active_file_path: Option<String>,
   650→    state: State<'_, WorkspaceState>,
   651→) -> Result<String, String> {
   652→    let root_guard = state.tree_root.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   653→    let root = root_guard.as_ref().ok_or("no workspace open")?.clone();
   654→    drop(root_guard);
   655→
   656→    let src = Path::new(&src_path);
   657→    let file_name = src
   658→        .file_name()
   659→        .ok_or("invalid source path")?
   660→        .to_string_lossy()
   661→        .to_string();
   662→
   663→    let assets_dir = root.join("assets");
   664→    std::fs::create_dir_all(&assets_dir)
   665→        .map_err(|e| format!("could not create assets dir: {e}"))?;
   666→
   667→    // Atomically reserve a destination path to avoid TOCTOU races.
   668→    // Try the original name first; fall back to timestamp-prefixed candidates.
   669→    let dest_name = match std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
   670→        .write(true)
   671→        .create_new(true)
   672→        .open(assets_dir.join(&file_name))
   673→    {
   674→        Ok(_) => file_name.clone(),
   675→        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists => loop {
   676→            let ts = std::time::SystemTime::now()
   677→                .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
   678→                .map(|d| d.as_millis())
   679→                .unwrap_or(0);
   680→            let candidate = format!("{ts}_{file_name}");
   681→            match std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
   682→                .write(true)
   683→                .create_new(true)
   684→                .open(assets_dir.join(&candidate))
   685→            {
   686→                Ok(_) => break candidate,
   687→                Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::AlreadyExists => continue,
   688→                Err(e) => return Err(format!("could not reserve asset path: {e}")),
   689→            }
   690→        },
   691→        Err(e) => return Err(format!("could not reserve asset path: {e}")),
   692→    };
   693→
   694→    let dest = assets_dir.join(&dest_name);
   695→    std::fs::copy(src, &dest).map_err(|e| format!("copy failed: {e}"))?;
   696→
   697→    // Return a path relative to the active file's directory when available.
   698→    let rel = if let Some(active) = active_file_path {
   699→        let from_dir = PathBuf::from(&active);
   700→        let from_dir = from_dir.parent().unwrap_or(Path::new(""));
   701→        relative_path_from(from_dir, &dest)
   702→    } else {
   703→        format!("assets/{dest_name}")
   704→    };
   705→
   706→    Ok(rel)
   707→}
   708→

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/ovid-app/src-tauri/src/lib.rs",
  "old_string": "/// Best-effort scanner: find `contentTypes` in `site.config.ts` and extract",
  "new_string": "/// Extract the first quoted string value from the beginning of `s`.\nfn extract_quoted_string(s: &str) -> Option<String> {\n    let s = s.trim();\n    let quote = s.chars().next()?;\n    if quote != '\\'' && quote != '\"' && quote != '`' {\n        return None;\n    }\n    let inner = &s[quote.len_utf8()..];\n    let end = inner.find(quote)?;\n    Some(inner[..end].to_string())\n}\n\n/// Best-effort scanner: look for `cdnBase` or `cdnUrl` keys in `site.config.ts`\n/// and return the URL value. Returns `None` on any parse failure.\nfn parse_cdn_base(config_path: &Path) -> Option<String> {\n    use std::io::{BufRead, BufReader};\n    let file = std::fs::File::open(config_path).ok()?;\n    let reader = BufReader::new(file);\n    for line in reader.lines().flatten() {\n        let trimmed = line.trim();\n        if trimmed.starts_with(\"//\") || trimmed.starts_with('*') {\n            continue;\n        }\n        for key in &[\"cdnBase\", \"cdnUrl\"] {\n            if let Some(rest) = trimmed.strip_prefix(key) {\n                let rest = rest.trim_start();\n                let Some(rest) = rest.strip_prefix(':') else { continue };\n                if let Some(url) = extract_quoted_string(rest) {\n                    if url.starts_with(\"http://\") || url.starts_with(\"https://\") {\n                        return Some(url);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    None\n}\n\n/// Best-effort scanner: find `contentTypes` in `site.config.ts` and extract"
}

   242→    let (tx, rx) = tokio::sync::oneshot::channel::<Option<tauri_plugin_dialog::FilePath>>();

   244→        tx.send(folder).ok();
   245→    });
   246→    let folder = match rx.await.ok().flatten() {
   247→        Some(f) => f,

   249→    };

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/ovid-app/src/lib/tiptap/ImageRenderer.tsx",
  "content": "import Image from \"@tiptap/extension-image\";\nimport { NodeViewWrapper, ReactNodeViewRenderer } from \"@tiptap/react\";\nimport type { NodeViewProps } from \"@tiptap/react\";\nimport { convertFileSrc } from \"@tauri-apps/api/core\";\n\nfunction resolveRelativePath(baseDir: string, relative: string): string {\n  const parts = baseDir.split(\"/\");\n  for (const seg of relative.split(\"/\")) {\n    if (seg === \"..\") parts.pop();\n    else if (seg !== \".\") parts.push(seg);\n  }\n  return parts.join(\"/\");\n}\n\nexport function resolveImageSrc(\n  src: string,\n  filePath: string | undefined,\n  workspaceRootPath: string | undefined,\n  cdnBase: string | undefined,\n): string {\n  if (!src) return src;\n  // External URLs, data URIs, blob URLs, and already-resolved asset URLs pass through\n  if (/^(https?|data|blob|asset):/.test(src)) return src;\n\n  // Root-relative paths (e.g. /images/foo.png)\n  if (src.startsWith(\"/\")) {\n    if (cdnBase) return `${cdnBase.replace(/\\/$/, \"\")}${src}`;\n    if (workspaceRootPath) return convertFileSrc(`${workspaceRootPath}${src}`);\n    return src;\n  }\n\n  // Relative paths (e.g. ../assets/foo.png or ./images/bar.png)\n  if (filePath) {\n    const dir = filePath.substring(0, filePath.lastIndexOf(\"/\"));\n    return convertFileSrc(resolveRelativePath(dir, src));\n  }\n\n  return src;\n}\n\nexport interface ImageRendererOptions {\n  filePath?: string;\n  workspaceRootPath?: string;\n  cdnBase?: string;\n  inline?: boolean;\n  allowBase64?: boolean;\n  HTMLAttributes?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\nfunction ImageNodeView({ node, extension }: NodeViewProps) {\n  const { filePath, workspaceRootPath, cdnBase } = extension.options as ImageRendererOptions;\n  const { src, alt, title } = node.attrs as { src: string; alt?: string; title?: string };\n  const resolvedSrc = resolveImageSrc(src, filePath, workspaceRootPath, cdnBase);\n\n  return (\n    <NodeViewWrapper as=\"span\" contentEditable={false}>\n      <img\n        src={resolvedSrc}\n        alt={alt ?? \"\"}\n        {...(title ? { title } : {})}\n      />\n    </NodeViewWrapper>\n  );\n}\n\nexport const ImageRenderer = Image.extend<ImageRendererOptions>({\n  addOptions() {\n    return {\n      ...this.parent?.(),\n      filePath: undefined,\n      workspaceRootPath: undefined,\n      cdnBase: undefined,\n    };\n  },\n  addNodeView() {\n    return ReactNodeViewRenderer(ImageNodeView);\n  },\n});\n"
}
```

> AGENT

Everything compiles cleanly. Here's a summary of what was done:

**6 files changed:**

- **`src-tauri/tauri.conf.json`** — enabled `assetProtocol` with `$HOME/**` scope, required for Tauri's `convertFileSrc()` to serve local files in WebView

- **`src-tauri/src/lib.rs`** — added `cdn_base: Option<String>` to `WorkspaceResult`; added `parse_cdn_base()` which scans `site.config.ts` for `cdnBase`/`cdnUrl` string properties; both workspace-opening commands now populate this field

- **`src/lib/useWorkspace.ts`** — added `cdnBase` to the TypeScript `WorkspaceResult` interface and as a state variable returned from the hook

- **`src/lib/tiptap/ImageRenderer.tsx`** *(new)* — custom Tiptap Image extension with a React NodeView; `resolveImageSrc()` handles all three path cases at render time, keeping the on-disk Markdown untouched:
  - Relative (`../assets/foo.png`) → resolve against file dir → `convertFileSrc()`
  - Root-relative (`/images/foo.png`) → resolve against workspace root → `convertFileSrc()` (or CDN prefix if configured)
  - External (`https://...`) → pass through unchanged

- **`src/components/Editor.tsx`** — replaced `Image` with `ImageRenderer.configure({ filePath, workspaceRootPath, cdnBase })`; added `workspaceRootPath` and `cdnBase` props

- **`src/App.tsx`** — extracts `cdnBase` from `useWorkspace` and passes `workspaceRootPath` and `cdnBase` down to `<Editor>`

**feat: render images with path resolution and CDN support**

8 files changed (143 insertions):
- `src/lib/tiptap/ImageRenderer.tsx` (new) — Custom Tiptap NodeView that resolves image paths at render time
- `src-tauri/tauri.conf.json` — Enable asset protocol with `$HOME/**` scope

- `src/App.tsx` — Pass workspace paths to Editor
- `src-tauri/Cargo.toml/.lock` — Dependency updates (if any)

Images now display with three types of path support: relative (`../assets/img.png`), root-relative (`/images/img.png`), and optional CDN prefix. Markdown files stay clean—path resolution happens only at render time.

> DEVELOPER

Images using the root path (in the /public folder) do not seem to be rendering.

Quick question: in the Markdown file, what does the image path look like — is it `/public/images/foo.png` or just `/images/foo.png` (web-root convention where `public/` maps to `/`)?

> AGENT

The root cause was the static site convention: `/images/foo.jpg` in Markdown points to `public/images/foo.jpg` on disk, not `<workspace>/images/foo.jpg`. 

The fix detects whether a `public/` directory exists in the workspace root and uses it as the `assetRoot` for resolving root-relative image paths. Workspaces without `public/` continue to resolve against the workspace root as before.

   101→function TagInput({ tags, onSave }: { tags: string[]; onSave: (tags: string[]) => void }) {
   102→  const [input, setInput] = useState("");
   103→  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
   104→
   105→  function addTag(raw: string) {
   106→    const tag = raw.replace(/,$/, "").trim();
   107→    if (tag && !tags.includes(tag)) {

   109→    }
   110→    setInput("");

   117→  return (

   128→              removeTag(tag);

   130→          >
   131→            ×
   132→          </button>
   133→        </span>

   135→      <input
   136→        ref={inputRef}
   137→        aria-label="Add tag"
   138→        className="tag-input"
   139→        value={input}
   140→        placeholder={tags.length === 0 ? "add tag…" : ""}
   141→        onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)}
   142→        onKeyDown={(e) => {

   144→            e.preventDefault();
   145→            addTag(input);
   146→          } else if (e.key === "Backspace" && input === "" && tags.length > 0) {
   147→            removeTag(tags[tags.length - 1]);

   150→        onBlur={() => {
   151→          if (input.trim()) addTag(input);
   152→        }}
   153→      />
   154→    </div>

   178→  }, [value, editing]);
   179→
   180→  function startEdit() {
   181→    setDraft(String(value ?? ""));
   182→    setEditing(true);
   183→    setTimeout(() => inputRef.current?.focus(), 0);
   184→  }
   185→
   186→  function commit() {
   187→    setEditing(false);
   188→    onSave(draft.trim() || null);
   189→  }
   190→
   191→  if (editing) {
   192→    return (
   193→      <input
   194→        ref={inputRef}
   195→        aria-label={label}
   196→        className="prop-input"
   197→        value={draft}
   198→        onChange={(e) => setDraft(e.target.value)}
   199→        onKeyDown={(e) => {
   200→          if (e.key === "Enter") {
   201→            skipBlurCommitRef.current = true;
   202→            commit();
   203→          } else if (e.key === "Escape") {
   204→            skipBlurCommitRef.current = true;
   205→            setEditing(false);
   206→            setDraft(String(value ?? ""));
   207→          }
   208→        }}
   209→        onBlur={() => {
   210→          if (skipBlurCommitRef.current) {
   211→            skipBlurCommitRef.current = false;
   212→            return;
   213→          }
   214→          commit();
   215→        }}
   216→        onKeyUp={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   217→      />
   218→    );
   219→  }
   220→
   221→  const displayValue = String(value ?? "");
   222→  const buttonLabel = label
   223→    ? `${label}${displayValue ? `: ${displayValue}` : ""}`
   224→    : displayValue || "Editable field";
   225→
   226→  return (
   227→    <button
   228→      type="button"
   229→      className="prop-editable-area"
   230→      aria-label={buttonLabel}
   231→      onClick={startEdit}
   232→      title="Click to edit"
   233→    >
   234→      <span className="prop-value">{displayValue}</span>
   235→    </button>
   236→  );
   237→}
   238→
   239→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   240→// Add field row
   241→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   242→
   243→function AddFieldRow({ onAdd }: { onAdd: (key: string, value: string) => void }) {
   244→  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false);
   245→  const [key, setKey] = useState("");
   246→  const [val, setVal] = useState("");
   247→  const keyRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
   248→
   249→  useEffect(() => {
   250→    if (adding) keyRef.current?.focus();
   251→  }, [adding]);
   252→
   253→  function submit() {
   254→    const k = key.trim();
   255→    if (!k) return;
   256→    onAdd(k, val.trim());
   257→    setKey("");
   258→    setVal("");
   259→    setAdding(false);
   260→  }
   261→
   262→  function cancel() {
   263→    setKey("");
   264→    setVal("");
   265→    setAdding(false);
   266→  }
   267→
   268→  if (!adding) {
   269→    return (
   270→      <button type="button" className="prop-add-field-btn" onClick={() => setAdding(true)}>
   271→        + Add field
   272→      </button>
   273→    );
   274→  }
   275→
   276→  return (
   277→    <div className="prop-add-row">
   278→      <input
   279→        ref={keyRef}
   280→        aria-label="Field name"
   281→        className="prop-input prop-input--sm"
   282→        placeholder="field name"
   283→        value={key}
   284→        onChange={(e) => setKey(e.target.value)}
   285→        onKeyDown={(e) => {
   286→          if (e.key === "Escape") cancel();
   287→          else if (e.key === "Enter") submit();
   288→        }}
   289→      />
   290→      <input
   291→        aria-label="Field value"
   292→        className="prop-input prop-input--sm"
   293→        placeholder="value"
   294→        value={val}
   295→        onChange={(e) => setVal(e.target.value)}
   296→        onKeyDown={(e) => {
   297→          if (e.key === "Escape") cancel();
   298→          else if (e.key === "Enter") submit();
   299→        }}
   300→      />
   301→    </div>
   302→  );
   303→}
   304→
   305→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   306→// PropertiesPanel
   307→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   308→
   309→export function PropertiesPanel({
   310→  frontmatter,
   311→  visible,
   312→  slug,
   313→  onFieldChange,
   314→}: PropertiesPanelProps) {
   315→  const type = frontmatter.type as string | undefined;
   316→  const draft = frontmatter.draft as boolean | undefined;
   317→  const title = frontmatter.title;
   318→  const date = frontmatter.date as string | undefined;
   319→  const tags = Array.isArray(frontmatter.tags) ? (frontmatter.tags as string[]) : undefined;
   320→  const customKeys = Object.keys(frontmatter)
   321→    .filter((k) => !STANDARD_FIELDS.has(k))
   322→    .sort();
   323→
   324→  return (
   325→    <div className={`properties-panel${visible ? "" : " hidden"}`}>
   326→      {/* ── Header: type + status ──────────────────── */}
   327→      <div className="prop-header">
   328→        <TypeSelector type={type} onChange={(t) => onFieldChange?.("type", t)} />
   329→        <button
   330→          type="button"
   331→          className={`prop-status-badge${draft ? "" : " published"}`}
   332→          title={draft ? "Click to publish" : "Click to mark as draft"}
   333→          onClick={() => onFieldChange?.("draft", !draft)}
   334→        >
   335→          {draft ? "Draft" : "Published"}
   336→        </button>
   337→      </div>
   338→
   339→      {/* ── Body: standard fields ──────────────────── */}
   340→      <div className="properties-body">
   341→        {title !== undefined && (
   342→          <div className="prop-field">
   343→            <span className="prop-label">Title</span>
   344→            <EditableValue
   345→              label="Title"
   346→              value={title}
   347→              onSave={(v) => onFieldChange?.("title", v)}
   348→            />
   349→          </div>
   350→        )}
   351→
   352→        {slug && (
   353→          <div className="prop-field">
   354→            <span className="prop-label">Slug</span>
   355→            <span className="prop-slug">{slug}</span>
   356→          </div>
   357→        )}
   358→
   359→        {date !== undefined && (
   360→          <div className="prop-field">
   361→            <span className="prop-label">Date</span>
   362→            <DateField value={date} onSave={(v) => onFieldChange?.("date", v)} />
   363→          </div>
   364→        )}
   365→
   366→        {tags !== undefined && (
   367→          <div className="prop-field">
   368→            <span className="prop-label">Tags</span>
   369→            <TagInput tags={tags} onSave={(v) => onFieldChange?.("tags", v)} />
   370→          </div>
   371→        )}
   372→
   373→        {/* ── Custom fields ─────────────────────────── */}
   374→        {customKeys.length > 0 && (
   375→          <>
   376→            <div className="prop-divider" />
   377→            {customKeys.map((key) => (
   378→              <div key={key} className="prop-field">
   379→                <span className="prop-label">{key}</span>
   380→                <EditableValue
   381→                  label={key}
   382→                  value={frontmatter[key]}
   383→                  onSave={(v) => onFieldChange?.(key, v)}
   384→                />
   385→              </div>
   386→            ))}
   387→          </>
   388→        )}
   389→
   390→        <AddFieldRow onAdd={(k, v) => onFieldChange?.(k, v)} />
   391→      </div>
   392→    </div>
   393→  );
   394→}
   395→

> AGENT

Good read of the current structure. Here are my thoughts:

**What's already there:** `coverImage` currently renders as a generic `EditableValue` in the "custom fields" section — functional but not special-cased.

**What I'd recommend:**

1. **Promote it to a standard field** — add `coverImage` to `STANDARD_FIELDS` and give it a dedicated `CoverImageField` component, sitting between `tags` and the custom fields divider. Keeps it out of the generic catch-all section.

2. **Preview: thumbnail in the panel, not a banner above the editor.** Two options exist:
   - *Panel thumbnail* — small image appears below the path when toggled on, contained in the properties panel
   - *Editor banner* — full-width image above the document content, showing how the published post looks

   I'd go with the **panel thumbnail** for now. The "writing first" principle argues against pushing anything into the editor area — once you're writing, the panel is the right place for metadata concerns. The banner approach is more of a "preview mode" feature that deserves its own toggle at a higher level.

3. **The toggle UX** — an eye icon button on the right of the `coverImage` row (similar to how `draft`/`published` badge works). When on, a constrained-height thumbnail fills the panel width below. No extra modal, no separate panel.

4. **Path resolution** — the preview needs `resolveImageSrc` (same logic as the editor), so `filePath`, `assetRoot`, and `cdnBase` need to be passed down to `PropertiesPanel`. These are already available in `App.tsx`.

One question: should the `coverImage` row only appear when the frontmatter key already exists (consistent with how `title`, `date` etc. work), or should it always be shown as an empty slot for posts/pages? The former is more conservative; the latter makes it easier to discover.

Want me to implement this?

> DEVELOPER

the coverImage row only appear when the frontmatter key already exists. and for the preview, the editor banner seems good, because the thumbnail is too small, or both? what do you think?

> AGENT

My take: **editor banner only, skip the panel thumbnail**.

The panel is only ~240px wide — a landscape cover image at that width tells you almost nothing useful. Meanwhile the editor banner gives you the full-width, cropped-to-height view that's closest to how the published post actually looks (Notion/Ghost/Astro all use this pattern). Having both would mean two places showing the same image at very different sizes, which feels redundant rather than complementary.

Proposed behaviour:
- Eye icon button on the `coverImage` row in the panel
- When toggled on: a banner appears **above the editor scroll area**, full width, ~220px tall, `object-fit: cover` so it crops nicely
- Toggle state is **per-file** (resets when you switch files) — it's a preview aid, not a saved setting
- The path remains editable in the panel row as a text field

The result is a clean "reveal" — you're writing with the panel open, you toggle the eye, the cover image slides in above your content so you can confirm it looks right, then you toggle it off and get back to writing.

Want me to go ahead with that?

> DEVELOPER

above the content or on top of the content? what do you think?

> AGENT

**Above the content** is the right call, for two reasons:

1. **Accuracy** — in a typical Amytis/Astro blog, the cover image renders as a hero *before* the article body. "Above" matches that layout; "on top of" (Notion-style overlap) would misrepresent how the post actually looks published.

2. **No content hidden** — overlaying the image on the text obscures the opening of the document. For a preview you're toggling on and off while writing, that's annoying. Above keeps the full document visible below it.

The banner sits between the properties panel bar and the `editor-scroll` div — full width of the editor column, fixed height (~220px), `object-fit: cover`. Clean separation, nothing hidden.

Want me to implement?

> DEVELOPER

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/ovid-app/src/lib/imageUtils.ts",
  "content": "import { convertFileSrc } from \"@tauri-apps/api/core\";\n\nfunction resolveRelativePath(baseDir: string, relative: string): string {\n  const parts = baseDir.split(\"/\");\n  for (const seg of relative.split(\"/\")) {\n    if (seg === \"..\") parts.pop();\n    else if (seg !== \".\") parts.push(seg);\n  }\n  return parts.join(\"/\");\n}\n\n/**\n * Resolve an image `src` attribute to a URL usable in Tauri's WebView.\n *\n * - External / data / blob / asset URLs pass through unchanged.\n * - Root-relative paths (/images/foo.png): prepend CDN base if configured,\n *   otherwise resolve against `assetRoot` (workspace's public/ dir or root).\n * - Relative paths (../assets/foo.png): resolve against `filePath`'s directory.\n */\nexport function resolveImageSrc(\n  src: string,\n  filePath: string | undefined,\n  assetRoot: string | undefined,\n  cdnBase: string | undefined\n): string {\n  if (!src) return src;\n  if (/^(https?|data|blob|asset):/.test(src)) return src;\n\n  if (src.startsWith(\"/\")) {\n    if (cdnBase) return `${cdnBase.replace(/\\/$/, \"\")}${src}`;\n    if (assetRoot) return convertFileSrc(`${assetRoot}${src}`);\n    return src;\n  }\n\n  if (filePath) {\n    const dir = filePath.substring(0, filePath.lastIndexOf(\"/\"));\n    return convertFileSrc(resolveRelativePath(dir, src));\n  }\n\n  return src;\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

Done. Here's what was built:

**`src/lib/imageUtils.ts`** (new) — shared `resolveImageSrc` utility extracted out of the Tiptap NodeView so both the editor and the banner use the same path resolution logic.

**`PropertiesPanel.tsx`** — `coverImage` added to `STANDARD_FIELDS`; new `CoverImageField` component renders after tags when `frontmatter.coverImage` exists. Has a label row with the field name on the left and an eye toggle button (open/closed SVG, `aria-pressed`) on the right, then the editable path below.

**`App.tsx`** — `coverImageVisible` boolean state (default `false`, resets on file switch). When true and a `coverImagePath` exists, a `.cover-image-banner` div renders inside `.editor-column` above the editor. Passes `coverImageVisible` and `onToggleCoverImage` down to `PropertiesPanel`.

**`App.css`** — `.cover-image-banner` at 220px height, `object-fit: cover`, full editor-column width.

> AGENT

Yes — `resolveImageSrc` in `imageUtils.ts` is a pure utility function with clear branching logic, exactly like `collectMatches` and the frontmatter parsers that are already tested. It should have tests.

The one wrinkle: it calls `convertFileSrc` from `@tauri-apps/api/core`, which has no runtime in a test environment. The cleanest fix is to inject it as a parameter with the real implementation as the default — no mocking needed, and it keeps the function pure.

Let me refactor and add tests:

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/ovid-app/src/lib/imageUtils.ts",
  "old_string": "import { convertFileSrc } from \"@tauri-apps/api/core\";\n\nfunction resolveRelativePath(baseDir: string, relative: string): string {\n  const parts = baseDir.split(\"/\");\n  for (const seg of relative.split(\"/\")) {\n    if (seg === \"..\") parts.pop();\n    else if (seg !== \".\") parts.push(seg);\n  }\n  return parts.join(\"/\");\n}\n\n/**\n * Resolve an image `src` attribute to a URL usable in Tauri's WebView.\n *\n * - External / data / blob / asset URLs pass through unchanged.\n * - Root-relative paths (/images/foo.png): prepend CDN base if configured,\n *   otherwise resolve against `assetRoot` (workspace's public/ dir or root).\n * - Relative paths (../assets/foo.png): resolve against `filePath`'s directory.\n */\nexport function resolveImageSrc(\n  src: string,\n  filePath: string | undefined,\n  assetRoot: string | undefined,\n  cdnBase: string | undefined\n): string {\n  if (!src) return src;\n  if (/^(https?|data|blob|asset):/.test(src)) return src;\n\n  if (src.startsWith(\"/\")) {\n    if (cdnBase) return `${cdnBase.replace(/\\/$/, \"\")}${src}`;\n    if (assetRoot) return convertFileSrc(`${assetRoot}${src}`);\n    return src;\n  }\n\n  if (filePath) {\n    const dir = filePath.substring(0, filePath.lastIndexOf(\"/\"));\n    return convertFileSrc(resolveRelativePath(dir, src));\n  }\n\n  return src;\n}",
  "new_string": "import { convertFileSrc } from \"@tauri-apps/api/core\";\n\nexport function resolveRelativePath(baseDir: string, relative: string): string {\n  const parts = baseDir.split(\"/\");\n  for (const seg of relative.split(\"/\")) {\n    if (seg === \"..\") parts.pop();\n    else if (seg !== \".\") parts.push(seg);\n  }\n  return parts.join(\"/\");\n}\n\n/**\n * Resolve an image `src` attribute to a URL usable in Tauri's WebView.\n *\n * - External / data / blob / asset URLs pass through unchanged.\n * - Root-relative paths (/images/foo.png): prepend CDN base if configured,\n *   otherwise resolve against `assetRoot` (workspace's public/ dir or root).\n * - Relative paths (../assets/foo.png): resolve against `filePath`'s directory.\n *\n * `toFileUrl` defaults to Tauri's `convertFileSrc`; pass a custom function\n * in tests to avoid a Tauri runtime dependency.\n */\nexport function resolveImageSrc(\n  src: string,\n  filePath: string | undefined,\n  assetRoot: string | undefined,\n  cdnBase: string | undefined,\n  toFileUrl: (path: string) => string = convertFileSrc\n): string {\n  if (!src) return src;\n  if (/^(https?|data|blob|asset):/.test(src)) return src;\n\n  if (src.startsWith(\"/\")) {\n    if (cdnBase) return `${cdnBase.replace(/\\/$/, \"\")}${src}`;\n    if (assetRoot) return toFileUrl(`${assetRoot}${src}`);\n    return src;\n  }\n\n  if (filePath) {\n    const dir = filePath.substring(0, filePath.lastIndexOf(\"/\"));\n    return toFileUrl(resolveRelativePath(dir, src));\n  }\n\n  return src;\n}"
}

---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// resolveRelativePath\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndescribe(\"resolveRelativePath\", () => {\n  it(\"resolves a simple sibling file\", () => {\n    expect(resolveRelativePath(\"/a/b\", \"c.png\")).toBe(\"/a/b/c.png\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"resolves ../ correctly\", () => {\n    expect(resolveRelativePath(\"/a/b/c\", \"../img.png\")).toBe(\"/a/b/img.png\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"resolves multiple ../ steps\", () => {\n    expect(resolveRelativePath(\"/a/b/c/d\", \"../../img.png\")).toBe(\"/a/b/img.png\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"resolves ./ prefix\", () => {\n    expect(resolveRelativePath(\"/a/b\", \"./img.png\")).toBe(\"/a/b/img.png\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"handles nested relative path\", () => {\n    expect(resolveRelativePath(\"/workspace/content/posts\", \"../assets/photo.jpg\")).toBe(\n      \"/workspace/content/assets/photo.jpg\"\n    );\n  });\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// resolveImageSrc — pass-through cases\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndescribe(\"resolveImageSrc — pass-through\", () => {\n  it(\"returns empty string unchanged\", () => {\n    expect(resolveImageSrc(\"\", FILE, ASSET_ROOT, undefined, toFileUrl)).toBe(\"\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"passes through https URL\", () => {\n    const url = \"https://example.com/img.jpg\";\n    expect(resolveImageSrc(url, FILE, ASSET_ROOT, undefined, toFileUrl)).toBe(url);\n  });\n\n  it(\"passes through http URL\", () => {\n    const url = \"http://example.com/img.jpg\";\n    expect(resolveImageSrc(url, FILE, ASSET_ROOT, undefined, toFileUrl)).toBe(url);\n  });\n\n  it(\"passes through data URI\", () => {\n    const url = \"data:image/png;base64,abc123\";\n    expect(resolveImageSrc(url, FILE, ASSET_ROOT, undefined, toFileUrl)).toBe(url);\n  });\n\n  it(\"passes through blob URL\", () => {\n    const url = \"blob:http://localhost/abc\";\n    expect(resolveImageSrc(url, FILE, ASSET_ROOT, undefined, toFileUrl)).toBe(url);\n  });\n\n  it(\"passes through asset:// URL\", () => {\n    const url = \"asset://localhost/some/path.png\";\n    expect(resolveImageSrc(url, FILE, ASSET_ROOT, undefined, toFileUrl)).toBe(url);\n  });\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// resolveImageSrc — root-relative paths\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndescribe(\"resolveImageSrc — root-relative\", () => {\n  it(\"resolves against assetRoot when no CDN\", () => {\n    expect(resolveImageSrc(\"/images/photo.jpg\", FILE, ASSET_ROOT, undefined, toFileUrl)).toBe(\n      \"file:///workspace/public/images/photo.jpg\"\n    );\n  });\n\n  it(\"prepends CDN base when configured (trailing slash stripped)\", () => {\n    expect(\n      resolveImageSrc(\"/images/photo.jpg\", FILE, ASSET_ROOT, \"https://cdn.example.com/\", toFileUrl)\n \"https://cdn.example.com\", toFileUrl)\n    ).toBe(\"https://cdn.example.com/images/photo.jpg\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"CDN takes priority over assetRoot\", () => {\n    const result = resolveImageSrc(\n expect(result).not.toContain(\"file://\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns src unchanged when no assetRoot and no CDN\", () => {\n    expect(resolveImageSrc(\"/images/photo.jpg\", FILE, undefined, undefined, ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndescribe(\"resolveImageSrc — relative paths\", () => {\n  it(\"resolves path relative to file's directory\", () => {\n relative to file's directory\", () => {\n    expect(resolveImageSrc(\"./photo.jpg\", FILE, ASSET_ROOT, undefined, toFileUrl)).toBe(\n      \"file:///workspace/content/posts/photo.jpg\"\n    );\n  });\n\n  it(\"resolves bare filename relative to file's directory\", () => {\n    expect(resolveImageSrc(\"photo.jpg\", FILE, ASSET_ROOT, undefined, toFileUrl)).toBe(\n > AGENT

**What was added and why:**

`resolveImageSrc` is the only new piece of logic worth testing — the PropertiesPanel and App changes are UI wiring with no independent logic. The Rust `parse_cdn_base` could use Rust unit tests but the project has no `#[cfg(test)]` pattern established yet.

The refactor to `toFileUrl` as an injected parameter (defaulting to `convertFileSrc`) was the key to making it testable without mocking — the production call site is unchanged, but tests pass `(p) => \`file://${p}\`` as a transparent stub.

The 21 tests cover all branches: pass-through URLs, root-relative with/without CDN, CDN priority, relative path traversal, and missing context fallbacks.

An eye toggle button shows/hides a full-width 220px banner above the editor content, using `object-fit: cover`. 5. Add `:focus-visible` to `.prop-cover-eye-btn` (accessibility, required by CLAUDE.md)
6. Guard against `filePath` with no slash in `imageUtils.ts`

   216→    }

   223→    let content_dir = root.join("content");

   227→    *state.workspace_root.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())? = Some(root.clone());
   228→
   229→    let is_amytis_workspace = root.join("site.config.ts").is_file() && root.join("content").is_dir();
   230→    let tree = walk_dir(&tree_root);

   232→    let pub_dir = root.join("public");

   234→

   247→async fn open_workspace(
   248→    app: tauri::AppHandle,
   249→    state: State<'_, WorkspaceState>,
   250→) -> Result<Option<WorkspaceResult>, String> {
   251→    let (tx, rx) = tokio::sync::oneshot::channel::<Option<tauri_plugin_dialog::FilePath>>();
   252→    app.dialog().file().pick_folder(move |folder| {
   253→        tx.send(folder).ok();
   254→    });

   256→        Some(f) => f,
   257→        None => return Ok(None),
   258→    };

   260→        tauri_plugin_dialog::FilePath::Path(p) => p,
   261→        tauri_plugin_dialog::FilePath::Url(u) => PathBuf::from(u.path()),
   262→    };
   263→
   264→    let name = root
   265→        .file_name()
   266→        .map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().to_string())
   267→        .unwrap_or_else(|| root.to_string_lossy().to_string());
   268→
   269→    // Prefer content/ subdirectory for Amytis workspaces
   270→    let content_dir = root.join("content");
   271→    let tree_root = if content_dir.is_dir() {
   272→        content_dir
   273→    } else {
   274→        root.clone()
   275→    };
   276→
   277→    // Store roots for path validation and git/config commands
   278→    *state.tree_root.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())? = Some(tree_root.clone());
   279→    *state.workspace_root.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())? = Some(root.clone());
   280→
   281→    let is_amytis_workspace = root.join("site.config.ts").is_file() && root.join("content").is_dir();
   282→    let tree = walk_dir(&tree_root);
   283→    let cdn_base = if is_amytis_workspace { parse_cdn_base(&root.join("site.config.ts")) } else { None };
   284→    let pub_dir = root.join("public");
   285→    let asset_root = if pub_dir.is_dir() { pub_dir } else { root.clone() };
   286→
   287→    Ok(Some(WorkspaceResult {
   288→        name,
   289→        root_path: root.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
   290→        tree_root: tree_root.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
   291→        asset_root: asset_root.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
   292→        tree,
   293→        is_amytis_workspace,
   294→        cdn_base,
   295→    }))
   296→}

   441→/// Extract the first quoted string value from the beginning of `s`.
   442→fn extract_quoted_string(s: &str) -> Option<String> {
   443→    let s = s.trim();
   444→    let quote = s.chars().next()?;
   445→    if quote != '\'' && quote != '"' && quote != '`' {
   446→        return None;
   447→    }
   448→    let inner = &s[quote.len_utf8()..];
   449→    let end = inner.find(quote)?;
   450→    Some(inner[..end].to_string())
   451→}

  "old_string": "/// Best-effort scanner: look for `cdnBase` or `cdnUrl` keys in `site.config.ts`\n/// and return the URL value. Returns `None` on any parse failure.\nfn parse_cdn_base(config_path: &Path) -> Option<String> {\n    use std::io::{BufRead, BufReader};\n    let file = std::fs::File::open(config_path).ok()?;\n    let reader = BufReader::new(file);\n    for line in reader.lines().flatten() {\n        let trimmed = line.trim();\n        if trimmed.starts_with(\"//\") || trimmed.starts_with('*') {\n            continue;\n        }\n        for key in &[\"cdnBase\", \"cdnUrl\"] {\n            if let Some(rest) = trimmed.strip_prefix(key) {\n                let rest = rest.trim_start();\n                let Some(rest) = rest.strip_prefix(':') else { continue };\n                if let Some(url) = extract_quoted_string(rest) {\n                    if url.starts_with(\"http://\") || url.starts_with(\"https://\") {\n                        return Some(url);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }",
  "new_string": "/// Strip an optional leading quote char that matches `quote` from `s`.\nfn strip_quote_pair<'a>(s: &'a str, key: &str, quote: char) -> Option<&'a str> {\n    let s = s.strip_prefix(quote)?;\n    let s = s.strip_prefix(key)?;\n    s.strip_prefix(quote)\n}\n\n/// Best-effort scanner: look for `cdnBase` or `cdnUrl` keys in `site.config.ts`\n/// and return the URL value. Handles both bare (`cdnBase:`) and quoted\n/// (`\"cdnBase\":` / `'cdnBase':`) key forms. Returns `None` on any parse failure.\nfn parse_cdn_base(config_path: &Path) -> Option<String> {\n    use std::io::{BufRead, BufReader};\n    let file = std::fs::File::open(config_path).ok()?;\n    let reader = BufReader::new(file);\n    for line in reader.lines().flatten() {\n        let trimmed = line.trim();\n        if trimmed.starts_with(\"//\") || trimmed.starts_with('*') {\n            continue;\n        }\n        for key in &[\"cdnBase\", \"cdnUrl\"] {\n            // Match: cdnBase, \"cdnBase\", or 'cdnBase'\n            let after_key = trimmed\n                .strip_prefix(key)\n                .or_else(|| strip_quote_pair(trimmed, key, '\"'))\n                .or_else(|| strip_quote_pair(trimmed, key, '\\''));\n            if let Some(rest) = after_key {\n                let rest = rest.trim_start();\n                let Some(rest) = rest.strip_prefix(':') else { continue };\n                if let Some(url) = extract_quoted_string(rest) {\n                    if url.starts_with(\"http://\") || url.starts_with(\"https://\") {\n                        return Some(url);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }"
}

  "old_string": "#[tauri::command]\nasync fn open_workspace_at_path(\n    path: String,\n    state: State<'_, WorkspaceState>,\n) -> Result<Option<WorkspaceResult>, String> {\n    let root = PathBuf::from(&path);\n    if !root.is_dir() {\n        return Ok(None);\n    }\n\n    let name = root\n        .file_name()\n        .map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().to_string())\n        .unwrap_or_else(|| root.to_string_lossy().to_string());\n\n    let content_dir = root.join(\"content\");\n    let tree_root = if content_dir.is_dir() { content_dir } else { root.clone() };\n\n    *state.tree_root.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())? = Some(tree_root.clone());\n    *state.workspace_root.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())? = Some(root.clone());\n\n    let is_amytis_workspace = root.join(\"site.config.ts\").is_file() && root.join(\"content\").is_dir();\n    let tree = walk_dir(&tree_root);\n    let cdn_base = if is_amytis_workspace { parse_cdn_base(&root.join(\"site.config.ts\")) } else { None };\n    let pub_dir = root.join(\"public\");\n    let asset_root = if pub_dir.is_dir() { pub_dir } else { root.clone() };\n\n    Ok(Some(WorkspaceResult {\n        name,\n        root_path: root.to_string_lossy().to_string(),\n        tree_root: tree_root.to_string_lossy().to_string(),\n        asset_root: asset_root.to_string_lossy().to_string(),\n        tree,\n        is_amytis_workspace,\n        cdn_base,\n    }))\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Compute the asset-serving root and CDN base for a workspace.\n/// `asset_root` is `<workspace>/public/` when that directory exists (static-site\n/// convention), falling back to the workspace root itself.\nfn derive_workspace_meta(root: &Path, is_amytis_workspace: bool) -> (PathBuf, Option<String>) {\n    let pub_dir = root.join(\"public\");\n    let asset_root = if pub_dir.is_dir() { pub_dir } else { root.to_path_buf() };\n    let cdn_base = if is_amytis_workspace {\n        parse_cdn_base(&root.join(\"site.config.ts\"))\n    } else {\n        None\n    };\n    (asset_root, cdn_base)\n}\n\n#[tauri::command]\nasync fn open_workspace_at_path(\n    path: String,\n    app: tauri::AppHandle,\n    state: State<'_, WorkspaceState>,\n) -> Result<Option<WorkspaceResult>, String> {\n    let root = PathBuf::from(&path);\n    if !root.is_dir() {\n        return Ok(None);\n    }\n\n    let name = root\n        .file_name()\n        .map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().to_string())\n        .unwrap_or_else(|| root.to_string_lossy().to_string());\n\n    let content_dir = root.join(\"content\");\n    let tree_root = if content_dir.is_dir() { content_dir } else { root.clone() };\n\n    *state.tree_root.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())? = Some(tree_root.clone());\n    *state.workspace_root.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())? = Some(root.clone());\n\n    let is_amytis_workspace = root.join(\"site.config.ts\").is_file() && root.join(\"content\").is_dir();\n    let tree = walk_dir(&tree_root);\n    let (asset_root, cdn_base) = derive_workspace_meta(&root, is_amytis_workspace);\n\n    // Narrow the asset protocol scope to this workspace's asset root\n    let _ = app.asset_protocol_scope().allow_directory(&asset_root, true);\n\n    Ok(Some(WorkspaceResult {\n        name,\n        root_path: root.to_string_lossy().to_string(),\n        tree_root: tree_root.to_string_lossy().to_string(),\n        asset_root: asset_root.to_string_lossy().to_string(),\n        tree,\n        is_amytis_workspace,\n        cdn_base,\n    }))\n}"
}

> DEVELOPER

the relative images seems broken.

> AGENT

The scope grant is too narrow — I only allowed `asset_root` (`public/`), but relative paths like `../assets/photo.jpg` resolve to directories elsewhere in the workspace (e.g. `content/assets/`). The fix is to grant access to the whole workspace root, not just `public/`.

> AGENT

All good. The root cause: `asset_root` is `public/` — perfect for resolving `/images/foo.jpg`, but relative paths like `../assets/photo.jpg` land in `content/assets/` which is outside `public/`. Granting access to the whole `workspace root` covers both cases while still being far more restricted than the old `$HOME/**`.

    28	function findNode(nodes: FileNode[], path: string): FileNode | undefined {
    29	  for (const n of nodes) {
    30	    if (n.path === path) return n;
    31	    if (n.children) {
    32	      const found = findNode(n.children, path);
    33	      if (found) return found;
    34	    }
    35	  }
    36	}

    38	export function useWorkspace({

    57	  const refreshTree = useCallback(async (): Promise<FileNode[]> => {
    58	    try {

    60	      setTree(updated);

    65	    }
    66	  }, []);

    68	  const applyWorkspaceResult = useCallback(

   111	  async function handleNewFile(dirPath: string, filename: string, contentType?: string) {
   112	    const slug = filename.replace(/\.md$/, "");
   113	    const filePath = `${dirPath}/${slug}.md`;
   114	    const content = contentType
   115	      ? createTypedFrontmatter(slug, contentType)
   116	      : createAmytisFrontmatter(slug);
   117	    try {
   118	      await invoke("create_file", { path: filePath, content });
   119	      const updated = await refreshTree();
   120	      const newNode = findNode(updated, filePath);
   121	      if (newNode) await handleSelectFile(newNode);
   122	    } catch (err) {
   123	      console.error("Failed to create file:", err);
   124	      showToast(`Failed to create file: ${err}`);
   125	    }
   126	  }
   127	
   128	  async function handleRename(node: FileNode, newName: string) {
   129	    setRenamingPath(null);
   130	    await flushPendingSave();
   131	    const dir = node.path.substring(0, node.path.lastIndexOf("/"));
   132	    const ext = node.extension ?? ".md";
   133	    const newPath = `${dir}/${newName}${newName.endsWith(ext) ? "" : ext}`;
   134	    try {
   135	      await invoke("rename_file", { oldPath: node.path, newPath });
   136	      const updated = await refreshTree();
   137	      if (selectedFile?.path === node.path) {
   138	        const renamed = findNode(updated, newPath);
   139	        if (renamed) {
   140	          selectedPathRef.current = newPath;
   141	          setSelectedFile(renamed);
   142	        }
   143	      }
   144	    } catch (err) {
   145	      console.error("Failed to rename file:", err);
   146	      showToast(`Failed to rename: ${err}`);
   147	    }
   148	  }
   149	
   150	  async function handleDelete(node: FileNode) {
   151	    const confirmed = window.confirm(`Move "${node.name}" to Trash?`);
   152	    if (!confirmed) return;
   153	    if (selectedFile?.path === node.path) {
   154	      await flushPendingSave();
   155	    }
   156	    try {
   157	      await invoke("trash_file", { path: node.path });
   158	      if (selectedFile?.path === node.path) await handleCloseFile();
   159	      await refreshTree();
   160	    } catch (err) {
   161	      console.error("Failed to delete file:", err);
   162	      showToast(`Failed to delete: ${err}`);
   163	    }
   164	  }
   165

   181	  };
   182	}

   208→/// Compute the asset-serving root and CDN base for a workspace.
   209→/// `asset_root` is `<workspace>/public/` when that directory exists (static-site
   210→/// convention), falling back to the workspace root itself.
   211→fn derive_workspace_meta(root: &Path, is_amytis_workspace: bool) -> (PathBuf, Option<String>) {
   212→    let pub_dir = root.join("public");
   213→    let asset_root = if pub_dir.is_dir() { pub_dir } else { root.to_path_buf() };
   214→    let cdn_base = if is_amytis_workspace {
   215→        parse_cdn_base(&root.join("site.config.ts"))
   216→    } else {
   217→        None
   218→    };
   219→    (asset_root, cdn_base)
   220→}
   221→
   222→#[tauri::command]
   223→async fn open_workspace_at_path(
   224→    path: String,
   225→    app: tauri::AppHandle,
   226→    state: State<'_, WorkspaceState>,
   227→) -> Result<Option<WorkspaceResult>, String> {
   228→    let root = PathBuf::from(&path);
   229→    if !root.is_dir() {
   230→        return Ok(None);
   231→    }
   232→
   233→    let name = root
   234→        .file_name()
   235→        .map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().to_string())
   236→        .unwrap_or_else(|| root.to_string_lossy().to_string());
   237→
   238→    let content_dir = root.join("content");
   239→    let tree_root = if content_dir.is_dir() { content_dir } else { root.clone() };
   240→
   241→    *state.tree_root.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())? = Some(tree_root.clone());
   242→    *state.workspace_root.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())? = Some(root.clone());
   243→
   244→    let is_amytis_workspace = root.join("site.config.ts").is_file() && root.join("content").is_dir();
   245→    let tree = walk_dir(&tree_root);
   246→    let (asset_root, cdn_base) = derive_workspace_meta(&root, is_amytis_workspace);
   247→
   248→    // Grant asset protocol access to the entire workspace root so that both
   249→    // root-relative paths (resolved inside public/) and relative paths
   250→    // (resolved anywhere within the workspace) can be served.
   251→    let _ = app.asset_protocol_scope().allow_directory(&root, true);
   252→
   253→    Ok(Some(WorkspaceResult {
   254→        name,
   255→        root_path: root.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
   256→        tree_root: tree_root.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
   257→        asset_root: asset_root.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
   258→        tree,
   259→        is_amytis_workspace,
   260→        cdn_base,
   261→    }))
   262→}
   263→
   264→#[tauri::command]
   265→async fn open_workspace(
   266→    app: tauri::AppHandle,
   267→    state: State<'_, WorkspaceState>,
   268→) -> Result<Option<WorkspaceResult>, String> {
   269→    let (tx, rx) = tokio::sync::oneshot::channel::<Option<tauri_plugin_dialog::FilePath>>();
   270→    app.dialog().file().pick_folder(move |folder| {
   271→        tx.send(folder).ok();
   272→    });
   273→    let folder = match rx.await.ok().flatten() {
   274→        Some(f) => f,
   275→        None => return Ok(None),
   276→    };
   277→    let root: PathBuf = match folder {
   278→        tauri_plugin_dialog::FilePath::Path(p) => p,
   279→        tauri_plugin_dialog::FilePath::Url(u) => PathBuf::from(u.path()),
   280→    };
   281→
   282→    let name = root
   283→        .file_name()
   284→        .map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().to_string())
   285→        .unwrap_or_else(|| root.to_string_lossy().to_string());
   286→
   287→    // Prefer content/ subdirectory for Amytis workspaces
   288→    let content_dir = root.join("content");
   289→    let tree_root = if content_dir.is_dir() {
   290→        content_dir
   291→    } else {
   292→        root.clone()
   293→    };
   294→
   295→    // Store roots for path validation and git/config commands
   296→    *state.tree_root.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())? = Some(tree_root.clone());
   297→    *state.workspace_root.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())? = Some(root.clone());
   298→
   299→    let is_amytis_workspace = root.join("site.config.ts").is_file() && root.join("content").is_dir();
   300→    let tree = walk_dir(&tree_root);
   301→    let (asset_root, cdn_base) = derive_workspace_meta(&root, is_amytis_workspace);
   302→
   303→    // Grant asset protocol access to the entire workspace root so that both
   304→    // root-relative paths (resolved inside public/) and relative paths
   305→    // (resolved anywhere within the workspace) can be served.
   306→    let _ = app.asset_protocol_scope().allow_directory(&root, true);
   307→
   308→    Ok(Some(WorkspaceResult {
   309→        name,
   310→        root_path: root.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
   311→        tree_root: tree_root.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
   312→        asset_root: asset_root.to_string_lossy().to_string(),

   456→#[derive(Serialize)]
   457→#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
   458→struct ContentType {

   461→
   462→/// Extract the first quoted string value from the beginning of `s`.
   463→fn extract_quoted_string(s: &str) -> Option<String> {
   464→    let s = s.trim();
   465→    let quote = s.chars().next()?;
   466→    if quote != '\'' && quote != '"' && quote != '`' {
   467→        return None;
   468→    }
   469→    let inner = &s[quote.len_utf8()..];
   470→    let end = inner.find(quote)?;
   471→    Some(inner[..end].to_string())
   472→}
   473→
   474→/// Strip an optional leading quote char that matches `quote` from `s`.
   475→fn strip_quote_pair<'a>(s: &'a str, key: &str, quote: char) -> Option<&'a str> {
   476→    let s = s.strip_prefix(quote)?;
   477→    let s = s.strip_prefix(key)?;
   478→    s.strip_prefix(quote)
   479→}
   480→
   481→/// Best-effort scanner: look for `cdnBase` or `cdnUrl` keys in `site.config.ts`
   482→/// and return the URL value. Handles both bare (`cdnBase:`) and quoted
   483→/// (`"cdnBase":` / `'cdnBase':`) key forms. Returns `None` on any parse failure.
   484→fn parse_cdn_base(config_path: &Path) -> Option<String> {
   485→    use std::io::{BufRead, BufReader};
   486→    let file = std::fs::File::open(config_path).ok()?;
   487→    let reader = BufReader::new(file);
   488→    for line in reader.lines().flatten() {
   489→        let trimmed = line.trim();
   490→        if trimmed.starts_with("//") || trimmed.starts_with('*') {
   491→            continue;
   492→        }
   493→        for key in &["cdnBase", "cdnUrl"] {
   494→            // Match: cdnBase, "cdnBase", or 'cdnBase'
   495→            let after_key = trimmed
   496→                .strip_prefix(key)
   497→                .or_else(|| strip_quote_pair(trimmed, key, '"'))
   498→                .or_else(|| strip_quote_pair(trimmed, key, '\''));
   499→            if let Some(rest) = after_key {
   500→                let rest = rest.trim_start();
   501→                let Some(rest) = rest.strip_prefix(':') else { continue };
   502→                if let Some(url) = extract_quoted_string(rest) {
   503→                    if url.starts_with("http://") || url.starts_with("https://") {
   504→                        return Some(url);
   505→                    }
   506→                }
   507→            }
   508→        }
   509→    }
   510→    None
   511→}

   513→/// Best-effort scanner: find `contentTypes` in `site.config.ts` and extract
