   573→fn extract_ts_key(line: &str) -> Option<String> {
   574→    let line = line.trim();
   575→    if line.starts_with("//") || line.starts_with('*') || line.starts_with("/*") {
   576→        return None;
   577→    }
   578→    let end = line.find(':')?;
   579→    let key = line[..end].trim().trim_matches('"').trim_matches('\'');
   580→    if !key.is_empty() && key.chars().all(|c| c.is_alphanumeric() || c == '_') {
   581→        Some(key.to_string())
   582→    } else {
   583→        None
   584→    }
   585→}
   586→
   587→#[tauri::command]
   588→fn get_content_types(state: State<'_, WorkspaceState>) -> Result<Vec<ContentType>, String> {
   589→    let root_guard = state.workspace_root.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   590→    let root = match root_guard.as_ref() {
   591→        Some(r) => r.clone(),
   592→        None => return Ok(Vec::new()),
   593→    };
   594→    drop(root_guard);
   595→    let config = root.join("site.config.ts");
   596→    if !config.is_file() {
   597→        return Ok(Vec::new());
   598→    }
   599→    Ok(parse_content_types(&config))
   600→}

   614→fn run_git(root: &str, args: &[&str]) -> Result<String, String> {

- `derive_workspace_meta` — public/ detection, workspace root fallback, CDN extraction for Amytis, CDN ignored for non-Amytis

The `imageUtils.ts` frontend tests were already comprehensive (22 tests) — no gaps there.

> AGENT

Here's the current state of image support in the app:

---

**What's already implemented**

| Method | How it works |
|---|---|
| **Drag & drop** | Drag image files from Finder onto the editor. Tauri copies them to `<workspace>/assets/` and inserts a relative `![name](../assets/file.png)` Markdown link. |
| **Typed Markdown** | Type `![alt](path)` directly in the editor — supports relative paths, root-relative `/images/…`, external `https://` URLs, and `data:` URIs. |

**What's NOT implemented yet**

- No toolbar button or keyboard shortcut to open a file picker for images  
- No way to edit an existing image's `src` or `alt` text in-place (clicking an image does nothing special)  
- No paste-from-clipboard for image data

---

So right now the only way to insert images is **drag & drop** from Finder, or **type the Markdown manually**. If you want a "Insert Image" button/shortcut or click-to-edit, that would need to be built. Want me to add one of those?

> DEVELOPER

insert image from menu, and support to edit the existeing image. what do you think?

> AGENT

Good additions. Here's my thinking before coding anything:

---

**Insert Image**

The app has no persistent toolbar, so the right affordance is a **keyboard shortcut** (`Cmd+Shift+I`) that opens Tauri's native file picker, copies the selected file to `assets/` via the existing `save_asset` command, then inserts the `![alt](path)` node. No new UI needed — consistent with the keyboard-first principle.

**Edit Existing Image**

`ImageRenderer.tsx` is already a React NodeView. When the user clicks an image, Tiptap sets a `NodeSelection` on it. We can detect that inside the NodeView and **conditionally render a small edit bar** (src + alt inputs) directly below the image — the same pattern as `CodeBlockView.tsx` which shows a language selector overlay. No portal, no extra dependencies.

The edit bar would:
- Show `src` and `alt` text fields pre-filled
- Update the node attrs on change (debounced or on blur)
- Dismiss on `Escape` or click outside

---

**What I'd build:**

| Feature | Approach |
|---|---|
| Insert | `Cmd+Shift+I` → Tauri file picker → `save_asset` → insert node |
| Edit | Click image → inline edit bar in the NodeView (src + alt) |
| Alt text for dropped images | Pre-fill alt from filename (already done) |

The two features are independent — I'd implement insert first (simpler), then the edit overlay. Want me to go ahead, or adjust the design?

> DEVELOPER

go ahead

Perfect. Here's a complete summary of the files you requested and the keyboard shortcut patterns found:

## Complete File Contents

### 1. `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/ovid-app/src/lib/tiptap/ImageRenderer.tsx`
A Tiptap extension for rendering images. It creates custom node views for image elements with image source resolution support (handles local paths, asset roots, and CDN bases).

### 2. `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/ovid-app/src/components/Editor.tsx`
The main Tiptap WYSIWYG editor component with:
- StarterKit with custom extensions (CodeBlockLowlight with CodeBlockView renderer, Markdown, Typography, Link, ImageRenderer, Table, Mathematics)
- Paste handling for URLs (applies as link marks)
- Drag-drop image support with async upload to workspace assets
- Multiple keyboard shortcut handlers via window.addEventListener('keydown'):
  - **Cmd+K** (Ctrl+K on non-Mac): Open link dialog when editor focused
  - **Cmd+E** (Ctrl+E): Toggle inline code
  - **Cmd+Shift+V** (Ctrl+Shift+V): Paste as plain text
  - **Cmd+H** (Ctrl+H): Toggle find & replace bar
- Event-forwarded menu actions (format-bold, format-italic, format-strike, format-code, format-heading-1/2/3, format-blockquote, format-bullet-list, format-ordered-list, insert-link, insert-code-block, insert-hr, insert-table)
- LinkDialog, FindReplaceBar, TableControls, and BubbleMenu components

### 3. `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/ovid-app/src/components/CodeBlockView.tsx`
A custom node view for code blocks with:
- Language selector dropdown (20 languages + plain option)
- Click-outside and Escape key handling to close the selector
- Pre-populated LANGUAGES array

### 4. `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/ovid-app/src/components/BubbleMenu.tsx`
A floating formatting toolbar that appears on text selection with buttons for:
- Bold (⌘B), Italic (⌘I), Strikethrough (no shortcut shown), Inline Code (⌘E), Link (⌘K)
- Uses onMouseDown with preventDefault to avoid losing selection
- Shows aria-pressed state and active class styling

## Keyboard Shortcut Patterns Found

**In Editor.tsx:**
All editor-level shortcuts use a consistent pattern:
```javascript
useEffect(() => {
  function onKeyDown(e: KeyboardEvent) {
    if (!(e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) || e.key?.toLowerCase() !== "k") return;
    if (!editor?.isFocused) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    // action here
  }
  window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
  return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
}, [editor]);
```

**In App.tsx:**
Global shortcuts use a centralized handler that checks for modifier keys (Cmd/Ctrl) and single key:
- **Escape**: Exits zen mode (checked first, before other guards)
- **Ctrl+Cmd+Z** (macOS only): Toggle zen mode
- **Cmd+Shift+P**: Toggle properties panel
- **Cmd+\\** (backslash): Toggle sidebar visibility
- **Cmd+O**: Open workspace
- **Cmd+Shift+O**: Open workspace switcher
- **Cmd+Shift+F**: Toggle full-text search panel
- **Cmd+Shift+G**: Open commit dialog (git repos only)
- **Cmd+P**: Open file switcher
- **Cmd+N**: Create new file
- **Cmd+S**: Save file
- **Cmd+W**: Close file

All shortcuts guard against operating when the focus is in an editable input/textarea/contentEditable element.

     1→import Image from "@tiptap/extension-image";
     2→import type { NodeViewProps } from "@tiptap/react";
     3→import { NodeViewWrapper, ReactNodeViewRenderer } from "@tiptap/react";
     4→import { resolveImageSrc } from "../imageUtils";
     5→
     6→export { resolveImageSrc };
     7→
     8→export interface ImageRendererOptions {
     9→  filePath?: string;
    10→  assetRoot?: string;
    11→  cdnBase?: string;
    12→  inline?: boolean;
    13→  allowBase64?: boolean;
    14→  HTMLAttributes?: Record<string, unknown>;
    15→}
    16→
    17→function ImageNodeView({ node, extension }: NodeViewProps) {
    18→  const { filePath, assetRoot, cdnBase } = extension.options as ImageRendererOptions;
    19→  const { src, alt, title } = node.attrs as { src: string; alt?: string; title?: string };
    20→  const resolvedSrc = resolveImageSrc(src, filePath, assetRoot, cdnBase);
    21→
    22→  return (
    23→    <NodeViewWrapper as="span" contentEditable={false}>
    24→      <img src={resolvedSrc} alt={alt ?? ""} {...(title ? { title } : {})} />
    25→    </NodeViewWrapper>
    26→  );
    27→}
    28→
    29→export const ImageRenderer = Image.extend<ImageRendererOptions>({
    30→  addOptions() {
    31→    return {
    32→      ...this.parent?.(),
    33→      filePath: undefined,
    34→      assetRoot: undefined,
    35→      cdnBase: undefined,
    36→    };
    37→  },
    38→  addNodeView() {
    39→    return ReactNodeViewRenderer(ImageNodeView);
    40→  },
    41→});

   137→          .filter((f): f is { name: string; srcPath: string } => f.srcPath !== undefined);
   138→        if (imageFiles.length === 0) return false;

   140→        // Capture coords now; recompute position after async uploads settle
   141→        // to avoid using a stale absolute offset if the document changes.
   142→        const dropX = event.clientX;

   158→          const dropPos = view.posAtCoords({ left: dropX, top: dropY })?.pos;
   159→          if (dropPos === undefined) return;

   161→          const tr = view.state.tr;
   162→          let offset = 0;

   351→      {editor && showFindReplace && (
   352→        <FindReplaceBar
   353→          editor={editor}
   354→          onClose={() => {
   355→            setShowFindReplace(false);
   356→            editor.chain().focus().run();
   357→          }}
   358→        />
   359→      )}
   360→      {editor && <TableControls editor={editor} />}
   361→      {editor && (
   362→        <BubbleMenu
   363→          editor={editor}
   364→          onLinkClick={() => {
   365→            const href = editor.getAttributes("link").href ?? "";
   366→            setLinkDialog({ href });
   367→          }}
   368→        />
   369→      )}
   370→      {linkDialog && (
   371→        <LinkDialog
   372→          initialHref={linkDialog.href}
   373→          onApply={(url) => {
   374→            editor?.chain().focus().extendMarkRange("link").setLink({ href: url }).run();
   375→            setLinkDialog(null);
   376→          }}
   377→          onRemove={() => {
   378→            editor?.chain().focus().extendMarkRange("link").unsetLink().run();
   379→            setLinkDialog(null);
   380→          }}
   381→          onCancel={() => setLinkDialog(null)}
   382→        />
   383→      )}
   384→    </div>
   385→  );
   386→}
   387→

The destination is safely\n/// constrained to `<workspace>/assets/`.\n#[tauri::command]\nfn save_asset("

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/ovid-app/src/lib/tiptap/ImageRenderer.tsx",
  "content": "import Image from \"@tiptap/extension-image\";\nimport type { NodeViewProps } from \"@tiptap/react\";\nimport { NodeViewWrapper, ReactNodeViewRenderer } from \"@tiptap/react\";\nimport { useEffect, useRef, useState } from \"react\";\nimport { resolveImageSrc } from \"../imageUtils\";\n\nexport { resolveImageSrc };\n\nexport interface ImageRendererOptions {\n  filePath?: string;\n  assetRoot?: string;\n  cdnBase?: string;\n  inline?: boolean;\n  allowBase64?: boolean;\n  HTMLAttributes?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\nfunction ImageNodeView({ node, extension, selected, updateAttributes }: NodeViewProps) {\n  const { filePath, assetRoot, cdnBase } = extension.options as ImageRendererOptions;\n  const { src, alt } = node.attrs as { src: string; alt?: string };\n\n  const [srcInput, setSrcInput] = useState(src);\n  const [altInput, setAltInput] = useState(alt ?? \"\");\n\n  // Keep refs so onBlur always reads the latest value regardless of render timing\n  const srcRef = useRef(srcInput);\n  const altRef = useRef(altInput);\n\n  const updateSrc = (v: string) => {\n    setSrcInput(v);\n    srcRef.current = v;\n  };\n  const updateAlt = (v: string) => {\n    setAltInput(v);\n    altRef.current = v;\n  };\n\n  // Sync from node attrs when not editing (undo/redo, external updates)\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!selected) {\n      setSrcInput(src);\n      srcRef.current = src;\n    }\n  }, [src, selected]);\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!selected) {\n      setAltInput(alt ?? \"\");\n      altRef.current = alt ?? \"\";\n    }\n  }, [alt, selected]);\n\n  const resolvedSrc = resolveImageSrc(srcInput, filePath, assetRoot, cdnBase);\n\n  return (\n    <NodeViewWrapper\n      as=\"span\"\n      contentEditable={false}\n      className={`image-node-wrapper${selected ? \" image-selected\" : \"\"}`}\n    >\n      <img src={resolvedSrc} alt={altInput} />\n      {selected && (\n        <div className=\"image-edit-bar\">\n          <div className=\"image-edit-field\">\n            <span className=\"image-edit-label\">src</span>\n            <input\n              className=\"image-edit-input\"\n              value={srcInput}\n              onChange={(e) => updateSrc(e.target.value)}\n              onBlur={() => updateAttributes({ src: srcRef.current })}\n              onKeyDown={(e) => {\n                e.stopPropagation();\n                if (e.key === \"Enter\") {\n                  updateAttributes({ src: srcRef.current });\n                  e.currentTarget.blur();\n                }\n              }}\n              aria-label=\"Image source path or URL\"\n              spellCheck={false}\n            />\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"image-edit-field\">\n            <span className=\"image-edit-label\">alt</span>\n            <input\n              className=\"image-edit-input\"\n              value={altInput}\n              onChange={(e) => updateAlt(e.target.value)}\n              onBlur={() => updateAttributes({ alt: altRef.current })}\n              onKeyDown={(e) => {\n                e.stopPropagation();\n                if (e.key === \"Enter\") {\n                  updateAttributes({ alt: altRef.current });\n                  e.currentTarget.blur();\n                }\n              }}\n              aria-label=\"Image alt text\"\n            />\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </NodeViewWrapper>\n  );\n}\n\nexport const ImageRenderer = Image.extend<ImageRendererOptions>({\n  addOptions() {\n    return {\n      ...this.parent?.(),\n      filePath: undefined,\n      assetRoot: undefined,\n      cdnBase: undefined,\n    };\n  },\n  addNodeView() {\n    return ReactNodeViewRenderer(ImageNodeView);\n  },\n});\n"
}

{

  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/ovid-app/src/components/Editor.tsx",
  "old_string": "const lowlight = createLowlight(common);\n\nconst IMAGE_MIME = /^image\\/(png|jpe?g|gif|webp|avif|svg\\+xml)$/;",
  "new_string": "const lowlight = createLowlight(common);\n\nconst IMAGE_MIME = /^image\\/(png|jpe?g|gif|webp|avif|svg\\+xml)$/;\n\nasync function pickAndInsertImage(\n  editor: ReturnType<typeof useEditor>,\n  filePath: string | undefined\n) {\n  if (!editor) return;\n  try {\n    const srcPath = await invoke<string | null>(\"pick_image_file\");\n    if (!srcPath) return;\n    const relPath = await invoke<string>(\"save_asset\", { srcPath, activeFilePath: filePath });\n    const fileName = (srcPath.split(\"/\").pop() ?? \"image\").replace(/\\.[^.]+$/, \"\");\n    editor.chain().focus().setImage({ src: relPath, alt: fileName }).run();\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error(\"insert image failed:\", err);\n  }\n}"
}

> AGENT

Done. Both features are now implemented:

---

**✓ Insert Image** (`Cmd+Shift+I` or **Insert > Image…** menu)
- Opens native file picker filtered to `.png`, `.jpg`, `.jpeg`, `.gif`, `.webp`, `.avif`, `.svg`
- Copies file to `<workspace>/assets/`
- Inserts with relative markdown path and auto-generated alt text (filename without extension)

**✓ Edit Image** (click to select)
- Inline edit bar appears below the selected image
- **src** field: edit the path/URL 
- **alt** field: edit accessibility text
- Updates commit on blur or Enter key
- Visual 2px accent outline around selected image
- Changes sync to markdown in real-time

Added `pick_image_file` Rust command for file dialog integration, enhanced `ImageRenderer.tsx` with React state + update handlers, and styled the edit bar with design tokens (accessible inputs, monospace paths).

     1→import Image from "@tiptap/extension-image";
     2→import type { NodeViewProps } from "@tiptap/react";
     3→import { NodeViewWrapper, ReactNodeViewRenderer } from "@tiptap/react";
     4→import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
     5→import { resolveImageSrc } from "../imageUtils";
     6→
     7→export { resolveImageSrc };
     8→
     9→export interface ImageRendererOptions {
    10→  filePath?: string;
    11→  assetRoot?: string;
    12→  cdnBase?: string;
    13→  inline?: boolean;
    14→  allowBase64?: boolean;
    15→  HTMLAttributes?: Record<string, unknown>;
    16→}
    17→
    18→function ImageNodeView({ node, extension, selected, updateAttributes }: NodeViewProps) {
    19→  const { filePath, assetRoot, cdnBase } = extension.options as ImageRendererOptions;
    20→  const { src, alt } = node.attrs as { src: string; alt?: string };
    21→
    22→  const [srcInput, setSrcInput] = useState(src);
    23→  const [altInput, setAltInput] = useState(alt ?? "");
    24→
    25→  // Keep refs so onBlur always reads the latest value regardless of render timing
    26→  const srcRef = useRef(srcInput);
    27→  const altRef = useRef(altInput);
    28→
    29→  const updateSrc = (v: string) => {
    30→    setSrcInput(v);
    31→    srcRef.current = v;
    32→  };
    33→  const updateAlt = (v: string) => {
    34→    setAltInput(v);
    35→    altRef.current = v;
    36→  };
    37→
    38→  // Sync from node attrs when not editing (undo/redo, external updates)
    39→  useEffect(() => {
    40→    if (!selected) {
    41→      setSrcInput(src);
    42→      srcRef.current = src;
    43→    }
    44→  }, [src, selected]);
    45→  useEffect(() => {
    46→    if (!selected) {
    47→      setAltInput(alt ?? "");
    48→      altRef.current = alt ?? "";
    49→    }
    50→  }, [alt, selected]);
    51→
    52→  const resolvedSrc = resolveImageSrc(srcInput, filePath, assetRoot, cdnBase);
    53→
    54→  return (
    55→    <NodeViewWrapper
    56→      as="span"
    57→      contentEditable={false}
    58→      className={`image-node-wrapper${selected ? " image-selected" : ""}`}
    59→    >
    60→      <img src={resolvedSrc} alt={altInput} />
    61→      {selected && (
    62→        <div className="image-edit-bar">
    63→          <div className="image-edit-field">
    64→            <span className="image-edit-label">src</span>
    65→            <input
    66→              className="image-edit-input"
    67→              value={srcInput}
    68→              onChange={(e) => updateSrc(e.target.value)}
    69→              onBlur={() => updateAttributes({ src: srcRef.current })}
    70→              onKeyDown={(e) => {
    71→                e.stopPropagation();
    72→                if (e.key === "Enter") {
    73→                  updateAttributes({ src: srcRef.current });
    74→                  e.currentTarget.blur();
    75→                }
    76→              }}
    77→              aria-label="Image source path or URL"
    78→              spellCheck={false}
    79→            />
    80→          </div>
    81→          <div className="image-edit-field">
    82→            <span className="image-edit-label">alt</span>
    83→            <input
    84→              className="image-edit-input"
    85→              value={altInput}
    86→              onChange={(e) => updateAlt(e.target.value)}
    87→              onBlur={() => updateAttributes({ alt: altRef.current })}
    88→              onKeyDown={(e) => {
    89→                e.stopPropagation();
    90→                if (e.key === "Enter") {
    91→                  updateAttributes({ alt: altRef.current });
    92→                  e.currentTarget.blur();
    93→                }
    94→              }}
    95→              aria-label="Image alt text"
    96→            />
    97→          </div>
    98→        </div>
    99→      )}
   100→    </NodeViewWrapper>
   101→  );
   102→}
   103→
   104→export const ImageRenderer = Image.extend<ImageRendererOptions>({
   105→  addOptions() {
   106→    return {
   107→      ...this.parent?.(),
   108→      filePath: undefined,
   109→      assetRoot: undefined,
   110→      cdnBase: undefined,
   111→    };
   112→  },
   113→  addNodeView() {
   114→    return ReactNodeViewRenderer(ImageNodeView);
   115→  },
   116→});
   117→

> AGENT

Here's my thinking on both:

**1. BubbleMenu suppression** — `TiptapBubbleMenu` accepts a `shouldShow` prop. An image click sets a `NodeSelection` (which has a `.node` property), while text selections don't. Simple duck-type check: `!("node" in selection)`.

**2. Edit bar refinement** — current design is two stacked rows which looks like a form. Better: a single compact horizontal bar with `SRC` and `ALT` side by side, divided by a hairline. Labels as tiny caps badges, inputs borderless (the bar container is the visual container), `src` in monospace (it's a path), `alt` in the UI font.

Let me implement both:

> AGENT

Done. Summary of changes:

**BubbleMenu** — `shouldShow` now returns false when `"node" in sel`, which is true for `NodeSelection` (image clicks, table node clicks) but never for `TextSelection`. Zero new imports needed.

**Image edit bar** — same visual language as the BubbleMenu itself:

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────┬─────────────────┐
│  SRC  ../assets/photo.jpg           │  ALT  Landscape │
└─────────────────────────────────────┴─────────────────┘
```
- 32px tall, single contained border + shadow
- SRC field is 2× wider than ALT (paths are long)
- Monospace for paths, UI font for alt text
- Inputs are muted until focused — accent color on focus

> DEVELOPER

The alt field doesn't seem wide enough. Would it be better to make the edit bar display like this: "![.....](........)" . what do you think?

> AGENT

That's a great idea — it mirrors the Markdown syntax `![alt](src)` directly, so the UI is self-documenting. No labels needed, both fields are visible at once, and it solves the width problem naturally (alt and src side by side with proportional sizing).

The bar becomes:

```
![ alt text here          ](  ../assets/photo.jpg            )
```

> AGENT

Done. The bar now reads exactly like a Markdown image reference — the `![`, `](`, `)` syntax tokens act as visual anchors so no separate labels are needed. The `alt` field gets `flex: 1` and the `src` field gets `flex: 2`, giving paths the room they need, and both are sized proportionally rather than fighting over a fixed layout.
